#version 330 core
precision highp float;
uniform sampler2D uBlur;
uniform vec2  uResolution;
uniform vec2  uBboxMin;
uniform vec2  uBboxSize;
const int MAX_RECTS = 64;
uniform int   uRectCount;
uniform vec4  uRects[MAX_RECTS];
uniform vec4  uRadii[MAX_RECTS];
uniform float uBrightness;
uniform vec4  uTint;
uniform float uEdge;
uniform float uShadowRadius;
uniform float uShadowStrength;
uniform float uShadowGamma;
uniform float uOpacity;
out vec4 frag;
float roundedBoxSDF4(vec2 p, vec2 halfSize, vec4 r) {
    float rad;
    if (p.x < 0.0) rad = (p.y > 0.0) ? r.x : r.w;
    else           rad = (p.y > 0.0) ? r.y : r.z;
    rad = min(rad, min(halfSize.x, halfSize.y));
    vec2 q = abs(p) - halfSize + vec2(rad);
    return length(max(q, 0.0)) - rad;
float unionSDF(vec2 pixelPos) {
    float d = 1e9;
    for (int i = 0; i < MAX_RECTS; i++) {
        if (i >= uRectCount) break;
        vec4 rc = uRects[i];
        vec2 halfSize = rc.zw * 0.5;
        vec2 center   = rc.xy + halfSize;
        d = min(d, roundedBoxSDF4(pixelPos - center, halfSize, uRadii[i]));
    return d;
void main() {
    vec2  p    = gl_FragCoord.xy;
    float dist = unionSDF(p);
    float shapeA  = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, uEdge, dist);
    float shadowA = 0.0;
    if (uShadowRadius > 0.001 && uShadowStrength > 0.001) {
        float outside = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, uShadowRadius, max(dist, 0.0));
        shadowA = pow(clamp(outside, 0.0, 1.0), uShadowGamma) * uShadowStrength * (1.0 - shapeA);
    float coverA = shapeA + shadowA;
    if (coverA <= 0.001) discard;
    vec2 uv    = (p - uBboxMin) / uBboxSize;
    vec4 b     = texture(uBlur, uv);
    vec3 glass = mix(b.rgb, uTint.rgb, clamp(uTint.a, 0.0, 1.0)) * uBrightness;
    vec3 rgb = mix(vec3(0.0), glass, shapeA / max(coverA, 0.001));
    /* @a pane that can actually fade out.
       uTint.a is NOT an opacity. It says how much of the tint colour is mixed
       into the blurred scene, so a caller fading a pane by winding it down to
       zero does not fade the pane at all 
 it strips the colour out and leaves
       the blurred scene composited at full coverage. That is an opaque frosted
       rectangle with nothing in it, which is exactly the bright smear a fading
       panel left standing for the whole length of its fade.
       Applied to the coverage, after the colour has been resolved, so the
       shape-versus-shadow ratio inside the pane is untouched by it. */
    float outA = coverA * clamp(uOpacity, 0.0, 1.0);
    if (outA <= 0.001) discard;
    frag = vec4(rgb * outA, outA);
#version 330 core
precision highp float;
uniform sampler2D uTex;
uniform vec2  uTexel;
uniform float uOffset;
in  vec2 vUv;
out vec4 frag;
void main() {
    vec2 o = uTexel * uOffset;
    vec4 c =
        texture(uTex, vUv + vec2(-o.x,  0.0)) +
        texture(uTex, vUv + vec2( o.x,  0.0)) +
        texture(uTex, vUv + vec2( 0.0, -o.y)) +
        texture(uTex, vUv + vec2( 0.0,  o.y)) +
        texture(uTex, vUv + vec2(-o.x, -o.y)) +
        texture(uTex, vUv + vec2( o.x, -o.y)) +
        texture(uTex, vUv + vec2(-o.x,  o.y)) +
        texture(uTex, vUv + vec2( o.x,  o.y));
    frag = c * (1.0 / 8.0);

    c += (texture(uTex, vUv + uDir * 1.0) + texture(uTex, vUv - uDir * 1.0)) * 0.144210;
    c += (texture(uTex, vUv + uDir * 2.0) + texture(uTex, vUv - uDir * 2.0)) * 0.115513;
    c += (texture(uTex, vUv + uDir * 3.0) + texture(uTex, vUv - uDir * 3.0)) * 0.079800;
    c += (texture(uTex, vUv + uDir * 4.0) + texture(uTex, vUv - uDir * 4.0)) * 0.047560;
    c += (texture(uTex, vUv + uDir * 5.0) + texture(uTex, vUv - uDir * 5.0)) * 0.024440;
    c += (texture(uTex, vUv + uDir * 6.0) + texture(uTex, vUv - uDir * 6.0)) * 0.010850;
    o = c;
#version 330
in vec2 vUv;
uniform sampler2D uBlur;
uniform sampler2D uMask;
uniform float uIntensity;
uniform float uInner;
out vec4 o;
void main() {
    vec4 b = texture(uBlur, vUv);
    float core = texture(uMask, vUv).a;
    vec3 col = (b.a > 0.001) ? (b.rgb / b.a) : vec3(0.0);
    float ring = clamp(b.a - core, 0.0, 1.0) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.35, 1.0, core));
    ring = pow(ring, 0.72);
    float inner = core * uInner * b.a;
    o = vec4(col * (ring * uIntensity + inner), 1.0);

#version 330 core
layout(location = 0) in vec2 aPos;
uniform vec2 uInvSize;
void main() {
    gl_Position = vec4(aPos * uInvSize * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
#version 330 core
layout(location = 0) in vec2 aPos;
uniform vec2 uMin;
uniform vec2 uMax;
out vec2 vUv;
void main() {
    vec2 t = aPos * 0.5 + 0.5;
    vUv = mix(uMin, uMax, t);
    gl_Position = vec4(vUv * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
#version 330 core
out vec4 frag;
void main() { frag = vec4(1.0); }
#version 330 core
uniform sampler2D uSrc;
uniform vec2  uTexel;
uniform float uR;
in  vec2 vUv;
out vec4 frag;
void main() {
    float far  = uR + 1.0;
    float best = far;
    for (int i = 0; i <= 24; ++i) {
        float fi = float(i);
        if (fi > uR) break;
        if (fi >= best) break;
        float hu = texture(uSrc, vec2(vUv.x, vUv.y + fi * uTexel.y)).r * far;
        float hd = texture(uSrc, vec2(vUv.x, vUv.y - fi * uTexel.y)).r * far;
        best = min(best, min(sqrt(hu * hu + fi * fi), sqrt(hd * hd + fi * fi)));
    frag = vec4(min(best, far) / far, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
#version 330 core
uniform sampler2D uMask;
uniform vec2  uTexel;
uniform float uR;
in  vec2 vUv;
out vec4 frag;
void main() {
    float far  = uR + 1.0;
    float best = far;
    if (texture(uMask, vUv).a > 0.5) {
        best = 0.0;
    } else {
        for (int i = 1; i <= 24; ++i) {
            float fi = float(i);
            if (fi > uR) break;
            float l = texture(uMask, vec2(vUv.x - fi * uTexel.x, vUv.y)).a;
            float r = texture(uMask, vec2(vUv.x + fi * uTexel.x, vUv.y)).a;
            if (max(l, r) > 0.5) { best = fi; break; }
        }
    frag = vec4(best / far, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
#version 330 core
uniform sampler2D uDist;
uniform float uFarPx;
uniform float uBiasPx;
uniform float uThick;
uniform float uGlow;
uniform float uGlowAlpha;
uniform vec4  uColor;
uniform int   uPass;      // 0 = halo, screened; 1 = line, over
in  vec2 vUv;
out vec4 frag;
void main() {
    /* The field is measured between texel CENTRES, so the first texel outside
       the figure already reports a whole texel of distance even though it is
       sitting on the edge. Removing half a texel is what puts the line against
       the figure instead of a gap away from it. */
    float d = max(0.0, texture(uDist, vUv).r * uFarPx - uBiasPx);
    /* Nothing is ever painted over the figure itself.
       Ramped over one framebuffer PIXEL, and it has to stay in pixels: d is
       already a true pixel distance at either buffer resolution, so rescaling
       this by the texel size would make it the one resolution-dependent term in
       the shader and open a visible step at the full/half switch where there is
       none today. */
    float outside = smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, d);
    /* Analytic coverage, and it also pays back a half pixel the line has been
       losing since it shipped. clamp(T + 1 - d, 0, 1) is the exact area of a
       one-pixel box on the lit side of a band edge at d = T; the +1 rather than
       +0.5 compensates `outside`, which integrates to half a pixel less than a
       step. Asked for 2.0 px the old expression laid down 1.5, and 0.8 gave
       0.355 
 every line in the panel's range was thinner than its number. */
    float line = outside * clamp(uThick + 1.0 - d, 0.0, 1.0);
    if (uPass != 0) {
        float a = line * uColor.a;
        /* One 8-bit level at the worst case. Below this the draw provably
           cannot move a byte, so it is fill spent for nothing. */
        if (a <= 0.004) discard;
        frag = vec4(uColor.rgb * a, a);
        return;
    if (uGlow <= 0.5) discard;
    /* @the halo is measured from the LINE's outer edge, not from the figure.
       Measured from the figure it sat UNDER the line, and how much survived
       depended on Thickness rather than on Glow size: at an eight-pixel line
       with an eight-pixel glow the entire halo was inside the line, and the
       0.121 that came through was the line's own antialiasing fringe. That is
       why the bottom half of Glow size did nothing at high Thickness. Rebased
       here it starts where the line's nominal width ends, at every combination.
       CUBED, not squared. The look being copied is a blurred silhouette, whose
       profile outside the edge is a complementary error function; against that
       reference the worst deviation across the band is 0.039 for t^3 against
       0.130 for the shipping t^2. Squared holds a quarter of its peak at the
       halfway point where the reference holds an eighth 
 nearly twice the
       light, spread flat through the middle, which is what reads as an even
       wash rather than as a light. Cubed lands on zero with zero slope, so
       there is no rim at the outer edge either.
       min() keeps the screen blend's boundedness true for any strength setting;
       (1 - line) keeps the outline one decal rather than two stacked, which
       matters because the colour carries a live alpha bar and a translucent
       line would otherwise come out brighter at its core than its own setting
       asks for. */
    float t = clamp(1.0 - max(0.0, d - uThick) / uGlow, 0.0, 1.0);
    float e = min(1.0, t * t * t * uGlowAlpha * uColor.a * outside * (1.0 - line));
    if (e <= 0.004) discard;
    frag = vec4(uColor.rgb * e, e);
